namespace = clever_dsk
clever_dsk.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.1.t
	desc = clever_dsk.1.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.1.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:university_benefactor
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_factory_opening"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		has_technology_researched = nitroglycerin
		NOT = {
			has_technology_researched = dynamite
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = dynamite_researcher
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = ecchi_swe_nobel_character_template
			ig_leader = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = dynamite_researcher
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_swe_nobel_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = university_benefactor
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.1.a
		trigger = {
		}
		add_technology_progress = {
			progress = 8000
			technology = dynamite
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = research_the_dynamite_modifier
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_small
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.2.t
	desc = clever_dsk.2.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.2.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:university_benefactor
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_trains"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		has_technology_researched = electrical_generation
		has_technology_researched = vulcanization
		has_technology_researched = shift_work
		NOT = {
			has_technology_researched = conveyors
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = conveyors_researcher
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = agitator_henry_ford
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = conveyors_researcher
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = agitator_henry_ford
			}
			save_scope_as = university_benefactor
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.2.a
		trigger = {
		}
		add_technology_progress = {
			progress = 8000
			technology = conveyors
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = research_the_conveyors_modifier
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_small
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.3.t
	desc = clever_dsk.3.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.3.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:university_benefactor
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/Imperial_Palace_normal.jpg"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		NOT = {
			has_variable = restore_summer_palace
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date > 1888.1.1
		game_date < 1901.1.1
		any_country = {
			NOT = {
				THIS = ROOT
			}
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_europe
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:han
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = chi_cixi_template
			ig_leader = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = restore_summer_palace
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = university_benefactor
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.3.a
		trigger = {
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = restore_summer_palace_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_medium
		}
		scope:university_benefactor ?= {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = happy_palace_restored
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.3.b
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = angry_palace_banned
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:university_benefactor ?= {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.4.t
	desc = clever_dsk.4.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.4.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:university_benefactor
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/Imperial_Palace_normal.jpg"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		NOT = {
			has_variable = kill_gong_qinwang
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date > 1878.1.1
		game_date < 1885.1.1
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = chi_gong_character_template
			ig_leader = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = kill_gong_qinwang
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = university_benefactor
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.4.a
		trigger = {
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:university_benefactor = {
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.4.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.5.t
	desc = clever_dsk.5.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.5.f
	duration = 1
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:university_benefactor
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/Imperial_Palace_normal.jpg"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		NOT = {
			has_variable = eight_country_war
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date > 1900.3.8
		game_date < 1901.9.17
		OR = {
			is_at_war = no
			has_war_with = c:JAP
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			is_in_government = yes
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = chi_cixi_template
			ig_leader = yes
		}
		any_country = {
			NOT = {
				THIS = ROOT
			}
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_europe
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:han
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = eight_country_war
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = university_benefactor
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.5.a
		trigger = {
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = china_fail_tt			#增加救亡图存的进度条
			je:je_china_19th_century = {
				add_progress = {
					value = 1
					name = china_reformist_progress_bar
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = southern_province_negative_modifier
			months = 14
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:GBR
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				#大清赔款定制代码
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:GBR
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:GBR
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:GBR
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:USA
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:USA
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:USA
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:USA
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:RUS
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:RUS
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:RUS
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:RUS
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:FRA
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:FRA
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:FRA
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:FRA
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:AUS
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:AUS
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:AUS
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:AUS
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:GER
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:GER
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:GER
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:GER
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:ITA
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:ITA
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:ITA
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHI
							target_country = c:ITA
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				exists = c:JAP
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = Chinese_Eight_war
					escalation = 94
					target_country = c:JAP
					type = dp_humiliation
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:JAP
						target_country = c:CHI
						type = enforce_treaty_article

							target_country = c:JAP

									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
                        if = {
                        limit = {
	                     is_player = no
                             is_subject = no
                        }
			trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.268
					days = 365
			}
                        }
			c:ITA ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.6
					popup = yes
				}
			}
			c:GER ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.6
					popup = yes
				}
			}
			c:JAP ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.6
					popup = yes
				}
			}
			c:AUS ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.6
					popup = yes
				}
			}
			c:USA ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.6
					popup = yes
				}
			}
			c:GBR ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.6
					popup = yes
				}
			}

					popup = yes
				}
			}

					popup = yes
				}
			}

	type = country_event

	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.6.a
		add_modifier = {
			name = preparing_for_war
			months = 1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.6.b
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				initiator_is = c:CHI
				target_is = ROOT
			}
			end_play = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.14 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.14.t
	desc = clever_dsk.14.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.14.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:university_sun_benefactor
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/roc.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = university_sun_benefactor
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.14.a
		trigger = {
		}
		create_character = {
			template = ecchi_chi_chen_jiongming_character_template
			ig_leader = yes
			interest_group = ig_landowners
		}
		create_character = {
			template = ecchi_chi_huang_xing_character_template
			is_general = yes
			ig_leader = yes
			interest_group = ig_intelligentsia
		}
		create_character = {
			template = ecchi_chi_cai_e_character_template
			is_general = yes
			interest_group = ig_armed_forces
		}
		create_character = {
			template = ecchi_chi_song_jiaoren_character_template
			ig_leader = yes
			interest_group = ig_industrialists
		}
		#委员长有1924年决议产生
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = clever_dsk.199
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.14.b
		scope:university_sun_benefactor = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bluff_called
				months = 36
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.15 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.15.t
	desc = clever_dsk.15.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.15.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:USA
		c:USA = ROOT
		country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
		game_date > 1917.1.1
		game_date < 1918.1.1
		exists = c:MEX
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		is_subject = no
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = zimmermann_telegrams
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = zimmermann_telegrams
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = france_need_help_scope
		}
		c:USA ?= {
			save_scope_as = american_give_help_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.15.a
		trigger = {
			scope:france_need_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:american_give_help_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:france_need_help_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:american_give_help_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			c:GER ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.50
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						c:USA ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:GER ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.20
					days = {
						1
						3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:france_need_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:american_give_help_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:france_need_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:american_give_help_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:france_need_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:american_give_help_scope
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.15.b
		s:STATE_ARIZONA = {
			add_claim = c:MEX
		}
		s:STATE_NEW_MEXICO = {
			add_claim = c:MEX
		}
		s:STATE_TEXAS = {
			add_claim = c:MEX
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.16 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.16.t
	desc = clever_dsk.16.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.16.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/balkfm_bulgaria_opener.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:BUL
		c:BUL = ROOT
		game_date > 1914.7.28
		game_date < 1917.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = revenge_against_serbia
		}
		is_subject = no
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:SER
		}
		c:SER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no		#保加利亚无法承担多场战争或博弈
		OR = {
			#大保加利亚！
			NOT = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_DOBRUDJA
			}
			NOT = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SKOPIA
			}
			NOT = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_WESTERN_THRACE
			}
			NOT = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_NORTHERN_THRACE
			}
			NOT = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_BULGARIA
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = revenge_against_serbia
		c:AUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = austria_need_help_scope
		}
		c:BUL ?= {
			save_scope_as = bulgaria_give_help_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.16.a
		add_modifier = {
			name = revenge_against_serbia_modifier
			months = 24
		}
		trigger = {
			scope:austria_need_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:bulgaria_give_help_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:austria_need_help_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:bulgaria_give_help_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_DOBRUDJA = {
			add_claim = c:BUL
		}
		s:STATE_SKOPIA = {
			add_claim = c:BUL
		}
		s:STATE_WESTERN_THRACE = {
			add_claim = c:BUL
		}
		s:STATE_NORTHERN_THRACE = {
			add_claim = c:BUL
		}
		s:STATE_BULGARIA = {
			add_claim = c:BUL
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:austria_need_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:bulgaria_give_help_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:austria_need_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:bulgaria_give_help_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:austria_need_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:bulgaria_give_help_scope
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						c:BUL ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.21
					days = {
						1
						3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.16.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			is_at_war = yes

			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}

			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
			has_war_with = c:GBR
			has_war_with = c:RUS

			}
		}

			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:austria_can_help_scope

					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:turkish_give_help_scope

					scope:turkish_give_help_scope

					target_is = scope:austria_can_help_scope

					scope:turkish_give_help_scope

	duration = 1
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		exists = c:ROC
		c:ROC = ROOT
		owns_entire_state_region = STATE_FUJIAN
		owns_entire_state_region = STATE_TIANSHAN
		NOT = {
			has_variable = built_more_cities
		}
		GDP >= 480000000
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = built_more_cities
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.25.a
		s:STATE_TIANSHAN = {
			add_resource_potential = building_logging_camp
			change_resource_potential = {
				resource = {
					type = "building_logging_camp"
					undiscovered_amount = 1
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_TIANSHAN.region_state:ROC = {
			create_building = {
				building = "building_logging_camp"
				level = 1
				reserves = 1
			}
		}
		s:STATE_FUJIAN = {
			add_resource_potential = building_coal_mine
			change_resource_potential = {
				resource = {
					type = "building_coal_mine"
					undiscovered_amount = 1
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_FUJIAN.region_state:ROC = {
			create_building = {
				building = "building_coal_mine"
				level = 1
				reserves = 1
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.25.b
		every_scope_state = {
			state_region = {
				add_pollution = -100
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.26 = {
	#借道比利时
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.26.t
	desc = clever_dsk.26.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.26.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:GER
		c:GER = ROOT
		game_date > 1914.5.1
		game_date < 1914.12.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = pass_the_belgium
		}
		exists = c:BEL
		exists = c:FRA
		exists = c:GBR
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		c:BEL ?= {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = c:GER
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = c:FRA
			}
		}
		is_at_war = yes
		any_neighbouring_state = {
			owner ?= {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:flemish
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:wallonian
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = pass_the_belgium
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.26.a
		trigger = {
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = -30
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = -30
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:BEL
			value = -50
		}
		change_infamy = 25
		hidden_effect = {
			c:BEL ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.27
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.26.b
		change_infamy = -5
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.27 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.27.t
	desc = clever_dsk.27.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.27.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = france_third_help_scope
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			save_scope_as = german_need_help_scope
		}
		c:BEL ?= {
			save_scope_as = belgium_give_help_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.27.a
		trigger = {
			scope:france_third_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = c:FRA
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:belgium_give_help_scope = {
			violate_sovereignty_join = {
				violator = c:GER
				target = ROOT
				join_violator = no
			}
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			trigger_event = {
				id = clever_dsk.29
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						c:BEL ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:GBR ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.28
					days = {
						1
						3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.27.b
		trigger = {
			scope:german_need_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:german_need_help_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:belgium_give_help_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:german_need_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:belgium_give_help_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:german_need_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:belgium_give_help_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:german_need_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:belgium_give_help_scope
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.29 = {
	#英国是否加入西线？
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.29.t
	desc = clever_dsk.29.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.29.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/1848_eur.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/worldwar.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = british_haha_help_scope
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = france_haha_help_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.29.a
		trigger = {
			scope:france_haha_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:british_haha_help_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:france_haha_help_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:british_haha_help_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:france_haha_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:british_haha_help_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:france_haha_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:british_haha_help_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:france_haha_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:british_haha_help_scope
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						c:GBR ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.42
					days = {
						1
						3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.29.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = british_lost_bel_prestige
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.29.c
		trigger = {
			scope:france_haha_help_scope = {
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:france_haha_help_scope
					is_war_participant = scope:british_haha_help_scope
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.30 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = war_crimes.1.t
	desc = war_crimes.1.d
	flavor = war_crimes.1.f
	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		exists = c:GER
		c:GER = ROOT
		exists = c:RUS
		exists = c:FRA
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		has_technology_researched = chemical_warfare		#化学战
		game_date > 1914.5.1
		game_date < 1918.1.1
		any_scope_general = {
			#任意将领配备毒气
			owner = ROOT
			this.commander_military_formation = {
				has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_war = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_front = {
					any_scope_general = {
						owner = ROOT
						this.commander_military_formation = {
							has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_front = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_general = {
						owner = ROOT
						this.commander_military_formation = {
							has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				random_scope_general = {
					limit = {
						owner = ROOT
						this.commander_military_formation = {
							has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = war_crimes_perpetrator
				}
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = war_crimes_state
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = war_crimes.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:war_crimes_perpetrator = {
			add_trait = war_criminal
		}
		scope:war_crimes_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = mustard_gas
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		change_infamy = 6
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					infamy < 94
				}
				add = 100
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.30.b
		change_infamy = -2
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					infamy > 94
				}
				add = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

		c:AUS ?= {

				}

				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:german_left_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:austria_left_help_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:german_left_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:austria_left_help_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:german_left_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:austria_left_help_scope
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.32.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.33 = {
	#希腊和土耳其人口互换
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.33.t
	desc = clever_dsk.33.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.33.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "asia_poor_people_moving"
	}
	cooldown = {
		years = 1
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_election.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:TUR
		exists = c:GRE
		c:TUR = ROOT
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		}
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		c:GRE ?= {
			relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tur_and_gre_pop_change
		}
		is_at_war = no
		any_scope_state = {
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = cu:greek
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = tur_and_gre_pop_change
			days = 365
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:greek
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = greek_remove_state
		}
		c:GRE ?= {
			save_scope_as = greek_territory_scope
			capital ?= {
				save_scope_as = athens_state
			}
		}
		cu:greek = {
			save_scope_as = greek_culture
		}
	}
	option = {
		#送走生活在土耳其的希腊人
		name = clever_dsk.33.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:greek_remove_state = {
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				culture = cu:greek
			}
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = cu:greek
				}
				move_pop = scope:athens_state
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GRE
			value = 35
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			c:GRE ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.34
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		#挽留希腊人当然这会激怒他们
		name = clever_dsk.33.b
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:greek
			value = 0.05
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.43 = {
	#英国附属国将参与东西线战争，前提是英国已经在战争中了，且这个点英法&英俄没有对立阵营打架，而且英国没有在教训附属国
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.43.t
	desc = clever_dsk.43.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.43.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:GBR
		game_date > 1914.7.28
		game_date < 1917.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = help_britain_mother
		}
		NOT = {
			c:GBR ?= this
		}
		lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
		owner ?= {
			is_subject_of = c:GBR
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			is_at_war = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = help_britain_mother
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = mother_need_our_help_scope
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = strong_father_need_help_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = grandmother_need_our_help_scope
		}
		owner ?= {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = c:GBR
			}
			save_scope_as = we_help_our_mother_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.43.a
		trigger = {
			scope:grandmother_need_our_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:mother_need_our_help_scope
				}
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:strong_father_need_help_scope
				}
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:mother_need_our_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:mother_need_our_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:mother_need_our_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:strong_father_need_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:strong_father_need_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:strong_father_need_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						THIS = scope:we_help_our_mother_scope
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.44
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		#如果不幸这个点英法、英俄其中一个还在互相打架，附属国表示中立
		name = clever_dsk.43.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = -20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.45 = {
	#剩余和平状态的次要政权以下非附属国家（不和平会过于混乱），加入协约国东西线战争
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.45.t
	desc = clever_dsk.45.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.45.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
		country_rank < rank_value:minor_power
		game_date > 1916.4.1		#还没打完？厉害的玩家已经结束了
		game_date < 1918.3.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = help_britain_french_russian
		}
		NOT = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_german
		}
		NOT = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:south_german
		}
		NOT = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
		}
		NOT = {
			c:GBR ?= this
			c:RUS ?= this
			c:FRA ?= this
			c:SER ?= this
			c:USA ?= this
			c:TUR ?= this
			c:BUL ?= this
			c:ITA ?= this
			c:ROM ?= this
			c:GER ?= this
			c:AUS ?= this
			c:BEL ?= this
			c:NET ?= this
			c:SWI ?= this
			c:SWE ?= this
			c:NOR ?= this
			c:DEN ?= this
			c:ICL ?= this
			c:GRN ?= this
		}
		is_subject = no
		is_revolutionary = no
		has_revolution = no
		lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
		NOT = {
			is_country_type = decentralized
			has_truce_with = c:GER
			has_truce_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = help_britain_french_russian
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = french_father_need_help_scope
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = russian_father_need_help_scope
		}
		owner ?= {
			save_scope_as = we_help_our_friends_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.45.a
		trigger = {
			scope:french_father_need_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
					}
				}
			}
			scope:russian_father_need_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:french_father_need_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:french_father_need_help_scope
					target_is = c:GER
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = c:GER
					target_is = scope:french_father_need_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:russian_father_need_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:russian_father_need_help_scope
					target_is = c:AUS
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = c:AUS
					target_is = scope:russian_father_need_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						THIS = scope:we_help_our_friends_scope
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.46
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = by_ww_one_get_prestige
			months = 12
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					c:POR ?= this
					c:NZL ?= this
					c:CAN ?= this
					c:SAF ?= this
					c:MON ?= this
					c:GRE ?= this
				}
				add = 250
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.45.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.48 = {
	#如果英国没参与东线，这个事件会让它参加，别让大毛一挑四了,快来达达尼尔海峡吧
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.48.t
	desc = clever_dsk.48.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.48.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:GBR
		c:GBR = ROOT
		game_date > 1914.3.1
		game_date < 1914.12.1
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = come_to_help_russia
		}
		NOT = {
			is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = c:RUS
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = come_to_help_russia
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = come_to_help_russian_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = britain_now_to_russian_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.48.a
		trigger = {
			scope:come_to_help_russian_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:britain_now_to_russian_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:come_to_help_russian_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:britain_now_to_russian_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						c:GBR ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.49
					days = {
						1
						3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:come_to_help_russian_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:britain_now_to_russian_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:come_to_help_russian_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:britain_now_to_russian_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:come_to_help_russian_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:britain_now_to_russian_scope
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.48.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}

clever_dsk.51 = {
	#考虑到1914这个点中华可能裂开了，那中华地区八千万人口以上且和平状态的tag会参与WW1，同时派遣劳工去法国
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.51.t
	desc = clever_dsk.51.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.51.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "asia_union_leader"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	trigger = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
		game_date > 1915.1.1
		game_date < 1918.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = help_european_people_alive
		}
		is_subject = no
		total_population > 88000000
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			is_at_war = yes
		}
		is_at_war = no
		any_scope_state = {
			state_population < 9000000
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = cu:han
				is_pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = help_european_people_alive
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = chinese_father_help_french_scope
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = chinese_father_help_russian_scope
		}
		owner ?= {
			save_scope_as = chinese_come_to_europe_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_population < 9000000
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:han
					is_pop_type = laborers
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = chinese_han_remove_state
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = frenchs_territory_scope
			capital ?= {
				save_scope_as = beautiful_paris_state
			}
		}
		cu:han = {
			save_scope_as = chinese_han_culture
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.51.a
		trigger = {
			scope:chinese_father_help_french_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
					}
				}
			}
			scope:chinese_father_help_russian_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:chinese_han_remove_state = {
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				culture = cu:han
				pop_type = laborers
			}
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = cu:han
					is_pop_type = laborers
				}
				move_pop = scope:beautiful_paris_state
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:chinese_father_help_french_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:chinese_father_help_french_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:chinese_father_help_french_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:chinese_father_help_russian_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:chinese_father_help_russian_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:chinese_father_help_russian_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
				}
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = 25
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = 30
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:RUS
			value = 20
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:SER
			value = 30
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						THIS = scope:chinese_come_to_europe_scope
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.52
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = by_ww_one_get_prestige
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.51.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}

		exists = c:ISR
		c:ISR = ROOT
		NOT = {
			exists = c:PAL
		}
		owns_entire_state_region = STATE_PALESTINE
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = angry_jewish_palestine
		}
		is_at_war = no
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = angry_jewish_palestine
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_has_state_religion = rel:jewish
			}
			ig:ig_devout = {
				set_interest_group_name = ig_rabbinical_council
				remove_ideology = ideology_moralist
				add_ideology = ideology_rabbinical_moralist
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.53.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_PALESTINE
			}
			s:STATE_PALESTINE = {
				add_homeland = cu:ashkenazi
				add_homeland = cu:sephardic
			}
		}
		create_country = {
			tag = PAL
			origin = this
			province = p:xC9CBC0
			on_created = {
				s:STATE_PALESTINE = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:PAL
						provinces = {
							x60D060
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.53.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_PALESTINE
			}
			s:STATE_PALESTINE = {
				add_homeland = cu:ashkenazi
				add_homeland = cu:sephardic
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:mashriqi
			value = 0.15
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.55 = {
	#原版列宁如果没出现，但RA列宁出现会触发，这会终结原版列宁出现的可能
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	title = historical_agitators.1.t
	desc = historical_agitators.1.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.1.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	#dlc = dlc004
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:RUS
				c:RUS ?= this
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SOV
				}
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:SOV
				c:SOV ?= this
				NOT = {
					exists = c:RUS
				}
			}
		}
		game_date > 1889.12.1
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = lenin_spawn
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = rus_lenin_character_template
			dna = ecchi_dna_vladimir_lenin
			traits = {
				persistent
				firebrand
				experienced_political_operator
				charismatic
				lawyer
				special_character_lenin_popularity
			}
			interest_group = ig_intelligentsia
			is_agitator = yes			#鼓动者
			on_created = {
				set_variable = lenin_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lenin_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = lenin_spawn
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			interest_group ?= {
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_lenin_in_exile
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.55.c
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.56 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:trotsky_state
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:historic_agitator_scope
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	title = historical_agitators.12.t
	desc = historical_agitators.12.d
	flavor = historical_agitators.12.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:RUS
				c:RUS ?= this
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SOV
				}
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:SOV
				c:SOV ?= this
				NOT = {
					exists = c:RUS
				}
			}
		}
		game_date > 1897.11.7
		game_date < 1917.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = trotsky_spawn
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = rus_leon_trotsky_character_template
			dna = ecchi_dna_leon_trotsky
			interest_group = ig_armed_forces
			ideology = ideology_trotskyist
			traits = {
				imperious
				erudite
				inspirational_orator
				bigoted
				special_character_blanqui_is_free
			}
			on_created = {
				set_variable = trotsky_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = trotsky_var
			}
			save_scope_as = historic_agitator_scope
		}
		set_global_variable = trotsky_spawn
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:ukrainian
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = trotsky_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = historical_agitators.12.a
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_trotsky_in_exile
				days = long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		scope:historic_agitator_scope = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.55.c
		default_option = yes
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:ukrainian
				}
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = 0.05
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.57 = {
	#第一次巴尔干战争，好几把都是塞尔维亚一个人孤零零单挑土耳其，我需要希腊、黑山、保加利亚帮助它
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.57.t
	desc = clever_dsk.57.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.57.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:GRE
		exists = c:SER
		exists = c:TUR
		c:GRE = ROOT
		game_date > 1912.5.1
		game_date < 1913.3.30
		c:SER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:TUR
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			has_war_with = c:SER
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_SKOPIA
					state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_SERBIA
					state_region = s:STATE_MACEDONIA
					state_region = s:STATE_THESSALIA
					state_region = s:STATE_WESTERN_THRACE
					state_region = s:STATE_DOBRUDJA
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTHERN_THRACE
					state_region = s:STATE_BULGARIA
					state_region = s:STATE_ALBANIA
					state_region = s:STATE_MONTENEGRO
					state_region = s:STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = c:TUR
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = greek_can_help_serbia
		}
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = c:TUR
		}
		#不是土耳其附属
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = greek_can_help_serbia
		c:SER ?= {
			save_scope_as = serbia_need_you_help_scope
		}
		c:GRE ?= {
			save_scope_as = greek_can_help_serbia_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.57.a
		trigger = {
			scope:serbia_need_you_help_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:greek_can_help_serbia_scope
				}
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:serbia_need_you_help_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:greek_can_help_serbia_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						c:GRE ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.60
					days = {
						1
						3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:serbia_need_you_help_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:greek_can_help_serbia_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:serbia_need_you_help_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:greek_can_help_serbia_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:serbia_need_you_help_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:greek_can_help_serbia_scope
				}
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:SER
			value = 20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.57.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:SER
			value = -10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.63 = {
	#1836-1936土耳其从没割让君堡，然而沙皇、希腊、拜占庭各路神仙，游戏里吃掉东色雷斯。英法应当保护土耳其（国际共管）
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.63.t
	desc = clever_dsk.63.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.63.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_battlefield_trenches"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	trigger = {
		game_date > 1867.1.1
		game_date < 1900.1.1
		exists = c:TUR
		exists = c:GBR
		exists = c:FRA
		NOT = {
			has_truce_with = c:TUR
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			is_at_war = no
			NOT = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
				}
			}
		}
		owns_entire_state_region = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
		NOT = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:turkish
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = give_some_thrace_to_turkish
		}
		NOT = {
			c:GBR ?= this
		}
		NOT = {
			c:FRA ?= this
		}
		NOT = {
			c:BYE ?= this
		}
		#拜占庭
		is_at_war = no
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = give_some_thrace_to_turkish
		owner ?= {
			save_scope_as = give_some_thrace_to_turkish_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.63.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
		}
		s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE = {
			set_owner_of_provinces = {
				country = c:TUR
				provinces = {
					xF0A020
					xF02082
					x194E90
					x2073F0
					x115006
				}
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = 20
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = 20
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:TUR
			value = 10
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
					}
					NOT = {
						THIS = scope:give_some_thrace_to_turkish_scope
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.64
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.63.b
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:turkish			#君堡那么多土耳其人，吃掉整个东色雷斯本身会激怒他们
			value = 0.1
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = -30
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = -30
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:TUR
			value = -30
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}

clever_dsk.65 = {
	#原版橡胶工人死亡事件，给比利时专属仅用一次，点不能放缓生产，几乎全球通报，恶名爆炸
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:amazon_state
	title = amazon.2.t
	desc = amazon.2.d
	flavor = amazon.2.f
	event_image = {
		video = "rp1_amazon_exploitation"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		exists = c:BEL
		c:BEL = ROOT
		NOT = {
			has_variable = bel_kill_drc_people
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				has_state_trait = state_trait_malaria
				has_state_trait = state_trait_amazon_rainforest
			}
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_rubber_plantation
			}
			NOR = {
				has_modifier = rubber_mistreatment
				has_modifier = rubber_slowdown
				has_modifier = rubber_protections
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_pop_type = laborers
						pop_is_discriminated = yes
					}
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}
				pop_employment_building = building_rubber_plantation
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = bel_kill_drc_people
		c:BEL ?= {
			save_scope_as = bel_kill_drc_people_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_trait = state_trait_malaria
					has_state_trait = state_trait_amazon_rainforest
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_rubber_plantation
				}
				NOR = {
					has_modifier = rubber_mistreatment
					has_modifier = rubber_slowdown
					has_modifier = rubber_protections
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_pop_type = laborers
							pop_is_discriminated = yes
						}
						is_pop_type = slaves
					}
					pop_employment_building = building_rubber_plantation
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = amazon_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_rubber_plantation
				}
				save_scope_as = rubber_plantation_scope
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_pop_type = laborers
							pop_is_discriminated = yes
						}
						is_pop_type = slaves
					}
					pop_employment_building = building_rubber_plantation
				}
				save_scope_as = rubber_laborers
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = amazon.2.a
		default_option = yes
		# greatly increase laborer mortality for rubber plantations
		scope:amazon_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rubber_mistreatment
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_russia
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_western_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_southern_europe
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_equatorial_africa
					}
					NOT = {
						THIS = scope:bel_kill_drc_people_scope
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.66
					days = {
						5
						7
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 98
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = amazon.2.b
		# greatly reduce throughput of rubber plantations
		scope:amazon_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rubber_slowdown
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = amazon.2.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_worker_protections
			}
		}
		# slightly reduce throughput of rubber plantations
		scope:amazon_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = rubber_protections
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

	#利奥波德二世试图获取刚果五块地，如果比利时AI一点刚果地盘都没有，只吃松散，与69互斥
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.68.t
	desc = clever_dsk.68.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.68.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:bel_leopold_II_saxe_gotha_scope
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:BEL
		c:BEL = ROOT
		NOT = {
			exists = c:AFS
		}
		has_game_rule = bantu_buff_on
		game_date > 1884.4.1
		game_date < 1908.1.1
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
			is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = leopold_II_want_kongol
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_BAS_CONGO
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KASAI
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KATANGA
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_EQUATEUR
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = leopold_II_want_kongol
		set_variable = scramble_for_africa_complete
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
			}
			save_scope_as = bel_leopold_II_saxe_gotha_scope
			add_trait = cruel
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.68.a
		default_option = yes
		activate_law = law_type:law_colonial_exploitation
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:BEL ?= this
			}
			s:STATE_BAS_CONGO = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner ?= {
							OR = {
								AND = {
									c:KON ?= this
									is_player = no
									lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
									OR = {
										is_subject = no
										is_subject_of = ROOT
									}
								}
								AND = {
									c:YKA ?= this
									is_country_type = decentralized
								}
							}
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:BEL
				}
				add_claim = c:BEL
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = establish_colony_kongol_modifier
			months = 48
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_large
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_player = yes
						c:FRA ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.70
					days = {
						7
						9
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.68.b
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = not_establish_colony_kongol
				months = 24
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}

		}

			value = -5
		}

clever_dsk.85 = {
	#孙先生去世，或者出现过孙先生但它不在中国，汪精卫会担任粤系小市民位置，接替他。与79互斥
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.79.t
	desc = clever_dsk.79.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.79.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	trigger = {
		game_date > 1920.1.1
		game_date < 1944.11.10
		exists = c:YUE
		c:YUE = ROOT
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		}
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = chi_sun_yat_sen_character_template
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = wangjingwei_become_new_leader
		}
		has_global_variable = sun_yat_sen_spawn
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = wangjingwei_become_new_leader
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.79.a
		trigger = {
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_wang_jingwei_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_wang_jingwei_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_wang_jingwei_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_wang_jingwei_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_wang_jingwei_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_chi_wang_jingwei_character_template
				ruler = yes
				ig_leader = yes
				on_created = {
					set_career_length = {
						years = 10
						random_range = {
							1
							1.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.79.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:chinese_culture
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = wuxu_bianfa_great
		}
		game_date > 1893.3.1
		game_date < 1899.1.1
		NOT = {
			any_state = {
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:han
				}
				NOT = {
					owner = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = wuxu_bianfa_great
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.89.a
		trigger = {
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
				ig_leader = yes
				on_created = {
					set_career_length = {
						years = 10
						random_range = {
							1
							1.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.89.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template

			}

		}

clever_dsk.91 = {
	#变为决议触发
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.91.t
	desc = clever_dsk.91.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.91.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:AUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = aus_want_annex_hun_scope
		}
		c:HUN ?= {
			save_scope_as = hun_never_die_scope
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = rus_help_aus_annex_hun_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.91.a
		trigger = {
			scope:aus_want_annex_hun_scope = {
				is_at_war = yes
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = scope:rus_help_aus_annex_hun_scope
				}
				has_war_with = scope:hun_never_die_scope
				any_scope_war = {
					is_warleader = scope:aus_want_annex_hun_scope
					NOT = {
						is_war_participant = scope:rus_help_aus_annex_hun_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:aus_want_annex_hun_scope
				NOT = {
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = scope:rus_help_aus_annex_hun_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator_is = scope:aus_want_annex_hun_scope
				}
				add_initiator_backers = {
					scope:rus_help_aus_annex_hun_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					target_is = scope:aus_want_annex_hun_scope
				}
				add_target_backers = {
					scope:rus_help_aus_annex_hun_scope
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.91.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:AUS
			value = -30
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

		c:CHI ?= {
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_TUVA
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = russian_move_tuva
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_population < 2300000
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:russian
					is_pop_type = aristocrats
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = russian_remove_state
		}
		s:STATE_TUVA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				save_scope_as = russian_tuva_state
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.97.a
		default_option = yes
		s:STATE_TUVA = {
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
		scope:russian_remove_state = {
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				culture = cu:russian
				pop_type = aristocrats
			}
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = cu:russian
					is_pop_type = aristocrats
				}
				move_pop = scope:russian_tuva_state
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			c:CHI ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.98
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.97.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}
