clever_dsk.102 = {
	#十四国干涉？
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.102.t
	desc = clever_dsk.102.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.102.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_battlefield_trenches"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/worldwar.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_rus_kolchak_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_rus_kolchak_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_rus_kolchak_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = c:SIB
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_rus_kolchak_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_rus_kolchak_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = c:SIB
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.102.a
		change_relations = {
			country = c:SIB
			value = 130
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:SIB ?= {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_TOMSK
					}
				}
			}
			s:STATE_TOMSK.region_state:SIB = {
				create_building = {
					building = "building_barrack"
					level = 5
					reserves = 1
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			c:SIB ?= {
				change_infamy = -100
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.102.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:SIB
			value = -20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.103 = {
	#俄罗斯AI变苏俄的全场通知
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.103.t
	desc = clever_dsk.103.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.103.f
	duration = 1
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = com_event_window_newspaper
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/rus3.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ideology_icons/ideology_leader/ideology_leader_vanguardist.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					exists = c:FIN
				}
			}
			create_country = {
				#创建国家
				tag = FIN				#芬兰
				origin = c:RUS
				state = s:STATE_WEST_KARELIA.region_state:RUS
				on_created = {
					#创建时
					activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy					#君主制，因为是德国傀儡
					every_diplomatic_play = {
						limit = {
							is_war = yes
							is_diplomatic_play_participant = c:GER
							NOT = {
								is_diplomatic_play_participant = c:FIN
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								initiator_is = c:GER
							}
							add_initiator_backers = {
								c:FIN
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								target_is = c:GER
							}
							add_target_backers = {
								c:FIN
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_WEST_KARELIA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:RUS ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:FIN
			}
		}
		s:STATE_ALAND = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:RUS ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:FIN
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KUOPIO = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:RUS ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:FIN
			}
		}
		s:STATE_OSTROBOTHNIA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:RUS ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:FIN
			}
		}
		s:STATE_OULU = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:RUS ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:FIN
			}
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = rus_now_very_red_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.103.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= {
					is_sucject = no
					is_in_power_bloc = no
				}
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				create_power_bloc = {
					name = Communist_International
					map_color = {
						1
						95
						55
					}
					founding_date = 1905.1.1					#游戏开局时间
					identity = identity_ideological_union					#意识形态联盟
					principle = principle_ideological_truth_3					#大后期阵营原则都是满级
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc_feature = power_bloc_features
					}
					power_bloc ?= {
						add_principle = principle_market_unification_3						#经互会
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc_feature = power_bloc_features
					}
					power_bloc ?= {
						add_principle = principle_defensive_cooperation_3						#华约
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc_feature = power_bloc_features
					}
					power_bloc ?= {
						add_principle = principle_military_industry_3						#军工业
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:RUS ?= {
						is_sucject = no
						power_bloc ?= {
							has_identity = identity:identity_sovereign_empire
						}
						is_power_bloc_leader = yes
					}
				}
				c:RUS ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_dlc_feature = power_bloc_features
						}
						power_bloc ?= {
							remove_principle = principle_construction_1
							remove_principle = principle_construction_2
							remove_principle = principle_construction_3
							remove_principle = principle_internal_trade_1
							remove_principle = principle_internal_trade_2
							remove_principle = principle_internal_trade_3
							remove_principle = principle_market_unification_1
							remove_principle = principle_market_unification_2
							remove_principle = principle_market_unification_3
							remove_principle = principle_advanced_research_1
							remove_principle = principle_advanced_research_2
							remove_principle = principle_advanced_research_3
							remove_principle = principle_defensive_cooperation_1
							remove_principle = principle_defensive_cooperation_2
							remove_principle = principle_defensive_cooperation_3
							remove_principle = principle_aggressive_coordination_1
							remove_principle = principle_aggressive_coordination_2
							remove_principle = principle_aggressive_coordination_3
							remove_principle = principle_external_trade_1

							remove_principle = principle_external_trade_3

							remove_principle = principle_divine_economics_2

							remove_principle = principle_police_coordination_3
							remove_principle = principle_transport_1
							remove_principle = principle_transport_2
							remove_principle = principle_transport_3

							remove_principle = principle_military_industry_2
							remove_principle = principle_military_industry_3
							remove_principle = principle_colonial_offices_1
							remove_principle = principle_colonial_offices_2

							remove_principle = principle_creative_legislature_2
							remove_principle = principle_creative_legislature_3
							remove_principle = principle_freedom_of_movement_1
							remove_principle = principle_freedom_of_movement_2
							remove_principle = principle_freedom_of_movement_3
							remove_principle = principle_divine_economics_1
							remove_principle = principle_divine_economics_2
							remove_principle = principle_divine_economics_3

							remove_principle = principle_exploit_members_2
							remove_principle = principle_exploit_members_3

							remove_principle = principle_sacred_civics_2
							remove_principle = principle_sacred_civics_3
							remove_principle = principle_ideological_truth_1
							remove_principle = principle_ideological_truth_2
							remove_principle = principle_ideological_truth_3
							remove_principle = principle_companies_1
							remove_principle = principle_companies_2
							remove_principle = principle_companies_3
							add_principle = principle_market_unification_3							#经互会
							add_principle = principle_defensive_cooperation_3							#华约
							add_principle = principle_military_industry_3							#军工业
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.103.e
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= {
					is_sucject = no
					is_in_power_bloc = no

				create_power_bloc = {
					name = Communist_International

						1
						95
						55
					}

					limit = {
						has_dlc_feature = power_bloc_features

						add_principle = principle_market_unification_3						#经互会
					}
				}

					}

							remove_principle = principle_colonial_offices_1

							remove_principle = principle_colonial_offices_3
							remove_principle = principle_foreign_investment_1

clever_dsk.104 = {
	#双洋条约
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.104.t
	desc = clever_dsk.104.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.104.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/unspecific_signed_contract.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/signed_contract"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	immediate = {
		c:CHL ?= {
			save_scope_as = chl_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.104.a
		default_option = yes
		c:ARG ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = c:CHL
				value = 10
			}
		}
		s:STATE_ARAUCANIA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:ARG
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:ARG ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:ARG
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:CHL
			}
		}
		s:STATE_ARAUCANIA = {
			remove_claim = c:ARG
		}
		s:STATE_PATAGONIA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:CHL
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:CHL ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:CHL
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:ARG
			}
		}
		s:STATE_PATAGONIA = {
			remove_claim = c:CHL
		}
		c:SLK ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_country_type = decentralized
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_ARAUCANIA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_ARAUCANIA = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:ARG
						provinces = {
							xB0E4AA
							xD2DD34
							x1BC85D
							xCC30AA
							x1205C7
							x62F8C0
							xAB76FA
							x350B0F
							xD88028
							x9806EE
							x977A70
							x78955F
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		c:CHL ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_ARAUCANIA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_ARAUCANIA = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:ARG
						provinces = {
							xB0E4AA
							xD2DD34
							x1BC85D
							xCC30AA
							x1205C7
							x62F8C0
							xAB76FA
							x350B0F
							xD88028
							x9806EE
							x977A70
							x78955F
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			s:STATE_ARAUCANIA = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner ?= {
							c:SLK ?= this
							is_country_type = decentralized
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:CHL
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.104.b
		c:ARG ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = c:CHL
				value = -30
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.118.t
	desc = clever_dsk.118.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.118.f
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_military_parade"
	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/ita.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:SAR ?= {
			save_scope_as = safuyi_will_unify_italy_scope
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = fra_will_help_safuyi_scope
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = fra_now_help_safuyi_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.118.a
		custom_tooltip = clever_dsk.118.a.ctt
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.118.b
		custom_tooltip = clever_dsk.118.b.ctt
	        trigger = {
                                c:FRA ?= this
	        }
		if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = c:SAR
							type = qwf_interest_alliance
						}
					        any_scope_treaty = {
							binds = c:SAR
							any_scope_article = {
								has_type = alliance
							}
					}
					}
				}
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = c:SAR
					type = qwf_interest_alliance
				}
				create_treaty = {
					name = plom2
					first_country = c:SAR
					second_country = c:FRA
					is_draft = no
					binding_period = {
						years = 1
					}
					articles_to_create = {
						{
							article = alliance
						}
					}
				}
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
}

					target_country = c:CHI

					target_country = c:LAN
				}

		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:LAN
			value = 10
		}

clever_dsk.125 = {
	#皇储尼古拉二世计划访问多国
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.125.t
	desc = clever_dsk.125.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.125.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:welcome_nicholas_ii_scope
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:RUS
		c:RUS = ROOT
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		heir ?= {
			has_template = RUS_nicholas_ii
		}
		game_date > 1890.10.1
		game_date < 1894.1.1
		is_at_war = no
		NOT = {
			has_variable = welcome_nicholas_ii
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = welcome_nicholas_ii
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = RUS_nicholas_ii
			}
			save_scope_as = welcome_nicholas_ii_scope
		}
		scope:welcome_nicholas_ii_scope = {
			remove_trait = well_travelled
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.125.a
		scope:welcome_nicholas_ii_scope = {
			add_trait = well_travelled
			add_trait = hunter
			set_character_immortal = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			c:CHI ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.126
					days = {
						5
						7
					}
				}
			}
			c:ROC ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.127
					days = {
						5
						7
					}
				}
			}
			c:JAP ?= {
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.128
					days = {
						5
						7
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.125.b
		scope:welcome_nicholas_ii_scope = {
			add_trait = rambler
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.137 = {
	#大久保利通御一新完成后出现
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.137.t
	desc = clever_dsk.137.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.137.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_europa_1_char_portrait
        event_image = {
		video = "ep2_intellectual_writer"
	}
	left_icon = scope:ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_scope
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:JAP
		c:JAP = ROOT
		game_date > 1856.1.1
		game_date < 1878.1.1
		has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = okubo_toshimichi_right_now
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = okubo_toshimichi_right_now
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
				ig_leader = no
		                on_created = {
				set_career_length = {
						years = 50
						random_range = { 1.0 1.5 }
				}
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
			}
			save_scope_as = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.137.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_scope = {
			set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			add_modifier = {
				name = ito_very_popular
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.14.b
		scope:ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bluff_called
				months = 12
			}
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.139 = {
	#智利拆邦联
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.139.t
	desc = clever_dsk.139.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.139.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:fight_with_bol_peu_scope
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:CHL
		c:CHL = ROOT
		game_date > 1836.1.10		#相信我，AI拆邦联倾向更快
		game_date < 1841.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = fight_with_bol_peu
		}
		lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
		is_subject = no
		OR = {
			c:NPU ?= {
				is_subject_of = c:BOL
			}
			c:IQU ?= {
				is_subject_of = c:BOL
			}
			c:SPU ?= {
				is_subject_of = c:BOL
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = fight_with_bol_peu
		c:BOL ?= {
			save_scope_as = bol_with_peu_con_scope
		}
		c:CHL ?= {
			save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
				name = war_reparations_amount
				value = {
					value = income
					multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = CHL_Diego_Portales
				}
				save_scope_as = fight_with_bol_peu_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.139.a
		default_option = yes
		c:ARG ?= {
			trigger_event = {
				id = clever_dsk.140
				days = {
					2
					5
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:NPU
				c:NPU ?= {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
			c:NPU ?= {
				every_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = PEU_Luis_Jose_de_Orbegoso
					}
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = PEU_Agustin_Gamarra
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = PEU_Agustin_Gamarra
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = PEU_Agustin_Gamarra
					}
					kill_character = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = PEU_Agustin_Gamarra
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = PEU_Agustin_Gamarra
				}
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHL
				exists = c:NPU
				c:NPU ?= {
					is_subject_of = c:BOL
				}
			}
			c:CHL ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHL ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					war = no
					target_country = c:NPU
					type = dp_liberate_subject
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:NPU
						target_country = c:CHL
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHL
							target_country = c:NPU
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHL
				exists = c:SPU
				c:SPU ?= {
					is_subject_of = c:BOL
				}
				c:CHL ?= {
					lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
				}
			}
			c:CHL ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:CHL ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					war = no
					target_country = c:SPU
					type = dp_liberate_subject
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = c:SPU
						target_country = c:CHL
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = money_transfer
							source_country = c:CHL
							target_country = c:SPU
							inputs = {
								{
									quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {

clever_dsk.157 = {
	#海地歧视西语加勒比人，还款西语人民还得一起承担，反了！
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.157.t
	desc = clever_dsk.157.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.157.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_springtime_of_nations"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:HAI
		c:HAI = ROOT
		has_journal_entry = je_haitian_debt
		game_date > 1844.2.1
		game_date < 1856.1.24
		NOT = {
			has_variable = independencia_of_dominicana
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = independencia_of_dominicana
		set_variable = se_viene_golpe_de_la_trinitaria
		trigger_event = {
			id = hai.10
			days = {
				1
				3
			}
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = hai.11
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = hai_angry_to_fra_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.157.a
		default_option = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						c:HAI ?= this
					}
					OR = {
						is_player = yes
						c:USA ?= this
						c:VNZ ?= this						#委内瑞拉历史上支持
						c:FRA ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = clever_dsk.158
					days = {
						3
						7
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:DOM
			}
		}
		create_country = {
			tag = DOM
			origin = c:HAI
			state = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO.region_state:HAI
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:HAI
				exists = c:DOM
			}
			c:HAI ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}
			c:HAI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					war = no
					target_country = c:DOM
					type = dp_annex_war
					add_war_goal = {
						#讲个笑话，1.9可以提出赔款要求后，再提出灭了他国的要求，那怎么拿赔款？
						holder = c:DOM
						type = regime_change
						target_country = c:HAI
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:caribeno
			value = 0.05
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = hai.1
			days = {
				8
				10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.157.b
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:caribeno
			value = 0.2
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.157.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:DOM
			}
		}
		create_country = {
			tag = DOM
			origin = c:HAI
			state = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO.region_state:HAI
		}
		create_diplomatic_pact = {
			country = c:DOM
			type = protectorate
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = hai.1
			days = {
				8
				10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}

clever_dsk.158 = {
	#圣多明各独立战争通知
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.158.t
	desc = clever_dsk.158.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.158.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_springtime_of_nations"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = ra_dom_against_hai_now
		c:DOM ?= {
			save_scope_as = dom_against_hai_scope
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_has_state_religion = rel:catholic
				}
				ig:ig_devout = {
					remove_ideology = ideology_moralist
					add_ideology = ideology_catholic_moralist
					remove_ideology = ideology_pious
					add_ideology = ideology_catholic_pious
				}
			}
		}
		c:HAI ?= {
			save_scope_as = hai_track_with_dom_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.158.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = c:DOM
			value = 60
		}
		c:DOM ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = funneling_weapons_modifier
				months = 16
			}
			add_loyalists = {
				culture = cu:caribeno
				value = 0.1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = DOM_juan_pablo_duarte
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = DOM_juan_pablo_duarte
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = DOM_juan_pablo_duarte
						}
						transfer_character = c:DOM
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
						set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = DOM_juan_pablo_duarte
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = DOM_juan_pablo_duarte
					}
					transfer_character = c:DOM
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_character = {
					template = DOM_juan_pablo_duarte
					ruler = yes
					ig_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:DOM ?= {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = ecchi_dom_salcedo_character_template
						}
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = ecchi_dom_polanco_character_template
						}
					}
				}
			}
			c:DOM ?= {
				create_character = {
					template = ecchi_dom_salcedo_character_template
					ig_leader = yes
					add_character_role = general
				}
				create_character = {
					template = ecchi_dom_polanco_character_template
					ig_leader = yes
					add_character_role = general
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO.region_state:DOM = {
			create_building = {
				#1.9版本多米尼加需要10个营才能打赢独立战争
				building = "building_barrack"
				level = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.158.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:DOM
			value = -30
		}
		change_infamy = 2
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

			base = 0

clever_dsk.164 = {
	#智利补偿玻利维亚
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.164.t
	desc = clever_dsk.164.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.164.f
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/unspecific_military_parade.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		exists = c:CHL
		c:CHL = ROOT
		exists = c:BOL
		game_date > 1836.12.12
		game_date < 1846.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = chl_compensate_peu
		}
		has_truce_with = c:BOL
		lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ANTOFAGASTA
				state_region = s:STATE_LIMA
				state_region = s:STATE_ICA
				state_region = s:STATE_CAJAMARCA
				state_region = s:STATE_AREQUIPA
				state_region = s:STATE_POTOSI
				state_region = s:STATE_SANTA_CRUZ
				state_region = s:STATE_LA_PAZ
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = chl_compensate_peu
                c:BRZ ?= {
			every_scope_war = {
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:BRZ
					value = 80
				}
			}
                }
		c:BOL ?= {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = chl_compensate_peu_ruler_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = chl_compensate_peu_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.164.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = c:BOL
			value = 80
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:BOL ?= {
					is_player = no
				}
				c:CHL ?= {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
			create_bidirectional_truce = {
				#50年的和平
				country = c:BOL
				months = 600
			}
		}
		c:BOL ?= {
			create_political_lobby = {
				type = lobby_pro_country
				target = c:CHL
				add_interest_group = ig:ig_industrialists
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = clever_dsk.165
				days = {
					2
					5
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.164.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:BOL
			value = -100
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.170 = {
	#马克西米利安当墨西哥皇帝
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = clever_dsk.170.t
	desc = clever_dsk.170.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.170.f
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:mex_hate_fra_now_scope
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		exists = c:MEX
		c:MEX = ROOT
		game_date > 1861.10.30
		game_date < 1900.1.1
		has_truce_with = c:FRA
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		has_modifier = forced_regime_change		#可恶，被政权更迭了
		NOT = {
			has_variable = mex_hate_fra_monarchy
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = mex_hate_fra_monarchy
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = aus_maximilian_I_habsburg_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = aus_maximilian_I_habsburg_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = aus_maximilian_I_habsburg_template
					}
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
					transfer_character = c:MEX
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = aus_maximilian_I_habsburg_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = aus_maximilian_I_habsburg_template
				}
				transfer_character = c:MEX
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			c:MEX ?= {
				create_character = {
					template = aus_maximilian_I_habsburg_template
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = is_Maximilian
					}
				}
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = mex_fra_aus_now_scope
		}
		c:MEX ?= {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = mex_hate_fra_now_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.170.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = 15
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:AUS
			value = 200
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.170.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:FRA
			value = -30
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

		exists = c:GBR
		c:GBR = ROOT
		game_date > 1865.8.14

		NOT = {
			has_variable = nile_david_livingstone_come
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = on_expedition
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = central_africa_expedition_success_var
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = ecchi_gbr_livingstone_character_template
			ig_leader = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = nile_david_livingstone_come
		set_variable = on_expedition
		c:GBR ?= {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_gbr_livingstone_character_template
				}
				save_scope_as = nile_david_livingstone_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.172.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = david_livingstone_find_nile_africa_modifier
			months = 12
		}
		scope:nile_david_livingstone_scope = {
			set_variable = expedition_leader_var
			set_character_busy_and_immortal = yes
			save_scope_as = expedition_leader
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = expedition_events.504
			days = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.172.b

		scope:nile_david_livingstone_scope = {

				name = disagree_explore_sad

			}

					culture = cu:russian

		}

						value = 0.1

						}
						NOT = {
							culture = cu:ukrainian
						}
						NOT = {
							culture = cu:byelorussian
						}
					}
				}
				move_pop = scope:beautiful_siberan_welcome_state
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = liufang_siberian_now
			months = 35
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.179.i
		scope:beautiful_siberan_arrive_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = 0.05
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75

		trigger = {

					NOT = {
						culture = cu:russian
					}
					NOT = {
						culture = cu:ukrainian
					}
					NOT = {
						culture = cu:byelorussian
					}
					strata = lower
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = USA_paying_removal_costs
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_small
			months = 35
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = liufang_siberian_now
			months = 35
		}
		scope:rus_ruler_make_people_love_siberian = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = kind
					}
				}
				add_trait = kind
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trait = cruel
				}
				remove_trait = cruel
			}
		}
		scope:beautiful_siberan_arrive_state = {
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							NOT = {
								culture = cu:russian
							}
							NOT = {
								culture = cu:ukrainian
							}
							NOT = {
								culture = cu:byelorussian
							}
							strata = lower
						}
						AND = {
							is_pop_type = farmers
							culture = cu:russian
						}
						AND = {
							is_pop_type = aristocrats
							culture = cu:russian
						}
					}
				}
				move_pop = scope:beautiful_siberan_welcome_state
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

		}

		scope:chi_junzhu_jieshou_benefactor = {
			add_trait = entrepreneur
		}

			limit = {
				c:CHI ?= this
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_EASTERN_HUBEI
			}
			s:STATE_EASTERN_HUBEI.region_state:CHI = {
				create_building = {
					building = "building_arms_industry"
					level = 5
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:ROC ?= this
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_EASTERN_HUBEI
			}
			s:STATE_EASTERN_HUBEI.region_state:ROC = {

					level = 5
				}
			}
		}

		}
	}

clever_dsk.182 = {
	#阿尔及利亚女英雄
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.182.t
	desc = clever_dsk.182.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.182.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_prison"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:FRA
		c:FRA = ROOT
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = solve_lalla_soumer
		}
		game_date > 1857.6.24
		game_date < 1863.9.30
		owns_entire_state_region = STATE_ALGIERS
		NOT = {
			exists = c:ALD
		}
		NOT = {
			exists = c:MAS
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = solve_lalla_soumer
		s:STATE_ALGIERS.region_state:FRA = {
			save_scope_as = solve_lalla_scope
		}
		cu:maghrebi = {
			save_scope_as = maghrebi_culture
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.182.a
		scope:solve_lalla_scope = {
			add_devastation = 25
		}
		trigger = {
		}
		scope:solve_lalla_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = 0.15
				culture = cu:maghrebi
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.15
			culture = cu:french
			pop_type = soldiers
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.15
			culture = cu:french
			pop_type = officers
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.182.b
		scope:solve_lalla_scope = {
			add_devastation = 10
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ald_general_nsoumer
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ald_general_nsoumer
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ald_general_nsoumer
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ald_general_nsoumer
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ald_general_nsoumer
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = ald_general_nsoumer
				is_general = yes
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = establish_catalan_modifier
			months = 12
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
		}
		scope:solve_lalla_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = establish_maghrebi_standard_of_living_modifier
				months = 12
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.05
			culture = cu:french
			pop_type = soldiers
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.15
			culture = cu:french
			pop_type = officers
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
}

	desc = clever_dsk.199.d

	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_3char_selection
	left_icon = scope:clever_dsk_199_character_1
	center_icon = scope:clever_dsk_199_character_2
	right_icon = scope:clever_dsk_199_character_3
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/roc.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = clever_dsk_199_character_1
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = clever_dsk_199_character_2
			}
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = clever_dsk_199_character_3
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.14.a
		trigger = {
		}
		scope:clever_dsk_199_character_1 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
			}
		}
		scope:clever_dsk_199_character_2 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
			}
		}
		scope:clever_dsk_199_character_3 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
			}
		}

			base = 100

	}

clever_dsk.200 = {
	#葡萄牙的萨拉查
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.200.t
	desc = clever_dsk.200.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.200.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_political_extremism.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:POR ?= this
			AND = {
				c:IBE ?= this
				was_formed_from = POR
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = por_salazar_happen_now
		}
		game_date > 1932.6.10
		game_date < 1936.1.1
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = por_salazar_happen_now
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.200.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
				ruler = yes
				ig_leader = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.200.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_por_antonio_salazar_character_template
					}
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {

clever_dsk.212 = {
	#保加利亚的斐迪南
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = bulgaria_events.2.t
	desc = bulgaria_events.2.d
	flavor = bulgaria_events.2.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "votp_monarch_holding_court"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/europenorthamerica/before_the_battle"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_bulgaria_major.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:BUL
				c:BUL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#保加利亚--南斯拉夫
				NOT = {
					exists = c:BUL
				}
				exists = c:YUG
				c:YUG = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BUL
			}
			AND = {
				#保加利亚--帝国
				NOT = {
					exists = c:BUL
				}
				exists = c:TSAR
				c:TSAR = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BUL
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1887.7.7
		game_date < 1918.1.1
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = BUL_ferdinand_i
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = BUL_boris_iii
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = bul_new_monarchy_now
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = bulgarian_independence
		set_global_variable = bul_new_monarchy_now
		create_character = {
			template = BUL_alexander_i
			on_created = {
				place_character_in_void = 24
			}
			save_scope_as = first_opt
		}
		create_character = {
			template = BUL_ferdinand_i
			on_created = {
				place_character_in_void = 24
			}
			save_scope_as = second_opt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BUL_stefan_stambolov
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = BUL_stefan_stambolov
				on_created = {
					place_character_in_void = 24
				}
				save_scope_as = third_opt
			}
		}
		ruler ?= {
			save_scope_as = bul_ruler
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = russia
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = bulgaria_events.2.b
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = modifier_regency
		ruler ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_immortal = yes
				}
				set_character_immortal = no
			}
		}
		ruler ?= {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
		create_character = {
			template = BUL_ferdinand_i
			ruler = yes
			heir = no
		}
		create_character = {
			template = BUL_boris_iii
			ruler = no
			heir = yes
		}
		remove_regency_modifier = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.212.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.215 = {
	#德国请求殖民地
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.215.t
	desc = clever_dsk.215.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.215.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:dissolve_william_II_want_africa_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "africa_construction_colony"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/ger_empire.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:FRA ?= this
			c:GBR ?= this
			c:POR ?= this
		}
		exists = c:GER
		game_date > 1884.4.1
		game_date < 1914.1.1
		c:GER ?= {
			is_player = no
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
		}
		has_game_rule = bantu_buff_on
		lca_is_in_diplomatic_play_or_war = no
		is_subject = no
		NOT = {
			has_variable = dissolve_william_II_want_africa
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_HEREROLAND
				state_region = s:STATE_NAMAQUALAND
				state_region = s:STATE_LINDI
				state_region = s:STATE_TANGANYIKA
				state_region = s:STATE_ZANZIBAR
				state_region = s:STATE_TOGO
				state_region = s:STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON
				state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_CAMEROON
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = dissolve_william_II_want_africa
		c:GER ?= {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = dissolve_william_II_want_africa_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.215.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GER
			value = 50
		}
		create_bidirectional_truce = {
			country = c:GER
			months = 120
		}
		add_treasury = 793650
		c:GER ?= {
			add_treasury = -793650
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:GER
				exists = c:GSAC
				c:GSAC ?= {
					is_subject_of = c:GER
				}
			}
			s:STATE_HEREROLAND = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner ?= {
							ROOT = THIS
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:GSAC
				}
			}
			s:STATE_NAMAQUALAND = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner ?= {
							ROOT = THIS
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:GSAC
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:GER
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GSAC
				}
			}
			s:STATE_HEREROLAND = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner ?= {
							ROOT = THIS
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:GER
				}
			}
			s:STATE_NAMAQUALAND = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner ?= {
							ROOT = THIS
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:GER
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

				exists = c:GEAC
				c:GEAC ?= {
					is_subject_of = c:GER
				}
			}
			s:STATE_LINDI = {
				every_scope_state = {

						}

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_automobile"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		game_date > 1836.2.1
		game_date < 1848.1.1
		capital ?= {
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_france_old
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = dsk_aw_character_template
			is_ruler_of_own_country = no
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = white_pretty_man_now
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = white_pretty_man_now
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = dsk_aw_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = white_pretty_man_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.216.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:white_pretty_man_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_embarassed_by_comrade
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.216.b
		ruler ?= {
			add_trait = special_character_el_excelentisimo
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = pru_general_von_roon
			}
			save_scope_as = 217_pru_general_von_roon_benefactor
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_smile
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_sad
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_proud
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_judao
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_angry
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_gongren
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_zhiyuan
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_jianyi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_nongmin
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_yinchen

			remove_variable = ecchi_27_shengbi

	left_icon = scope:zaitian_become_cixi_son_scope
	right_icon = scope:chi_cixi_become_zaitian_mother_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "Imperial_Palace_winter"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/china.dds"
	trigger = {
		game_date > 1874.1.1
		game_date < 1905.1.1
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
				is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
			}
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
				is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
			}
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = chi_daoguang_template
				is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
			}
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
			is_heir_of_own_country = yes
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			#慈禧在的
			has_template = chi_cixi_template
			#ig_leader = yes
			#is_ruler_of_own_country = no
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = chi_cixi_become_zaitian_mother
		}
		OR = {
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_landowners
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_devout
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_petty_bourgeoisie
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_industrialists
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_trade_unions
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_intelligentsia
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_armed_forces
			has_modifier = modifier_regency_rural_folk
			has_modifier = modifier_regency
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			#司马P社再政变一个试试
			name = coup_cooldown_var
			days = 4380
		}
		set_global_variable = chi_cixi_become_zaitian_mother
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = chi_cixi_template
			}
			save_scope_as = chi_cixi_become_zaitian_mother_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
			}
			save_scope_as = zaitian_become_cixi_son_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.218.a
		default_option = yes
		ruler ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_immortal = yes
				}
				set_character_immortal = no
			}
		}
		scope:chi_cixi_become_zaitian_mother_scope = {
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_immortal = no
				}
				set_character_immortal = yes
			}
		}
		scope:zaitian_become_cixi_son_scope = {
			add_trait = political_appointee
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.218.b
		scope:chi_cixi_become_zaitian_mother_scope = {
			add_trait = wrathful
		}
		scope:zaitian_become_cixi_son_scope = {
			remove_trait = political_appointee
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.226 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.226.t
	desc = clever_dsk.226.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.226.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:dsk_jap_sir_bread_dutch_character_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
					}
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
				is_general = yes
				is_agitator = no
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = dsk_jap_sir_bread_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = dsk_jap_sir_bread_dutch_character_scope
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_smile
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_yinchen
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_proud
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_chensi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_judao
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_angry
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_gongren
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_jianyi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_nongmin
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_zhiyuan
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_shengbi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kongbu
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_pibei
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kelian
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_sad
				days = 365
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.226.a
		scope:dsk_jap_sir_bread_dutch_character_scope = {
			set_character_immortal = no
			kill_character = yes
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			strata = upper
			value = small_radicals
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

		game_date > 1915.3.1
		game_date < 1917.1.1
		c:TUR ?= {
			is_subject = no
			is_player = no
			has_variable = turkish_angry
			has_war_with = c:RUS
			has_war_with = ROOT
			any_scope_state = {
				#汉志
				state_region = s:STATE_HEDJAZ
			}
		}
		exists = c:GBR
		c:GBR = ROOT
		is_subject = no
		c:RUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:AUS
		}
		c:AUS ?= {
			has_war_with = c:RUS
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			#劳伦斯
			has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_come_to_arabic
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_come_to_arabic
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_scope
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_smile
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_yinchen
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_proud
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_chensi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_sad
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_angry
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_gongren
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_jianyi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_nongmin
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_zhiyuan
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_shengbi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kongbu
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_pibei
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kelian
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_judao
				days = 365
			}
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_lawrence_attack_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.227.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				exists = c:HDJ
			}
			c:TUR ?= {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HEDJAZ
				}
			}
		}
		create_country = {
			tag = HDJ
			origin = c:TUR
			state = s:STATE_HEDJAZ.region_state:TUR
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:TUR
				c:HDJ ?= {
					owns_entire_state_region = STATE_HEDJAZ
				}
			}
			s:STATE_HEDJAZ = {
				set_owner_of_provinces = {
					country = c:TUR
					provinces = {
						xCD06B6
						xEEB4B7
						xE4A66C
						xA8EF8C
						xE4415E
						xD128E6
						x320615
						x3BE5E7
						x4F8C15
						xBBA7D8
						x1BC05F
					}
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_HEDJAZ = {
			add_claim = c:HDJ
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = huguo_war
				escalation = 30
				type = dp_annex_war
				target_country = c:HDJ
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:HDJ
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_HEDJAZ.region_state:TUR
				}
			}
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				initiator_is = c:TUR
				target_is = c:HDJ
			}
			add_target_backers = {
				c:GBR
			}
			add_war_goal = {
				holder = c:GBR
				type = humiliation
				target_country = c:TUR
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_player = yes
					c:TUR ?= this
				}
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = clever_dsk.228
				days = 7
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = lawrence_leave_now_tt
			c:HDJ ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_country = {
							any_scope_character = {
								has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
							}
						}
					}
					random_country = {
						limit = {
							any_scope_character = {
								has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
							}
						}
						random_scope_character = {
							limit = {
								has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
							}
							transfer_character = c:HDJ
							add_character_role = general
							set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						any_character_in_exile_pool = {
							has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
						}
					}
					random_character_in_exile_pool = {
						limit = {
							has_template = ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_template
						}
						transfer_character = c:HDJ
						add_character_role = general
						set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.227.b
		scope:ecchi_gbr_lawrence_character_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bluff_called
				months = 36
			}
		}

			base = 0
		}
	}

			}
		}

			}
		}

			base = 100
		}
	}

			name = fra_lose_prestige_in_europe
			months = 6
		}

			value = 0.05
			culture = cu:french

clever_dsk.237 = {
	#凡尔登战役
	type = country_event
	placement = s:STATE_LORRAINE.region_state:FRA
	title = clever_dsk.237.t
	desc = clever_dsk.237.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.237.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:FRA
	minor_right_icon = c:GER
	gui_window = event_window_highlander
	left_icon = scope:ger_Battle_of_Verdun_benefactor
	right_icon = scope:fra_Battle_of_Verdun_benefactor
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/ger_empire2.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:FRA ?= this
			c:GER ?= this
			is_player = yes
		}
		game_date > 1915.1.1
		game_date < 1917.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = Battle_of_Verdun
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
			any_scope_character = {
				OR = {
					has_template = pru_general_von_moltke
					has_template = pru_general_falkenhayn
					has_template = 27_ger_kronprinz_template
				}
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_LORRAINE
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_LORRAINE
				devastation >= 0.001
			}
			any_scope_war = {
				any_scope_front = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_LORRAINE
					}
				}
			}
			has_war_with = c:GER
			any_scope_character = {
				OR = {
					has_template = fra_general_joffre
					has_template = fra_general_foch
					has_template = ecchi_fra_petain_character_template
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = Battle_of_Verdun
		c:GER ?= {
			save_scope_as = ger_Battle_of_Verdun
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_template = pru_general_von_moltke
						has_template = pru_general_falkenhayn
						has_template = 27_ger_kronprinz_template
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = ger_Battle_of_Verdun_benefactor
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = fra_Battle_of_Verdun
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_template = fra_general_joffre
						has_template = fra_general_foch
						has_template = ecchi_fra_petain_character_template
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = fra_Battle_of_Verdun_benefactor
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.237.a
		c:GER ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = Artillery_Battle_of_Verdun
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = Artillery_Battle_of_Verdun
					months = 10
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {

					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = Artillery_Battle_of_Verdun
					months = 10
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

clever_dsk.240 = {
	#婚礼
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.205.t
	desc = clever_dsk.205.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.205.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_2char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:the_dsk_scope
	right_icon = scope:the_louis_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_airplane"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes		#玩家
		has_global_variable = com_internal_prestige_leader_old
		game_date > 1838.1.1
		game_date < 1840.1.1
		has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_equatorial_africa
		c:LND ?= {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
			}
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = dsk_scholar_character_marry
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = {
			name = dsk_scholar_character_marry
		}
		c:LND ?= {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
				}
				save_scope_as = the_dsk_scope
				remove_variable = ecchi_27_jianyi
				set_variable = {
					name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
					months = 1
				}
			}
		}
		c:LND ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
						}
						transfer_character = c:LND
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = c:LND
				}
			}
		}
		c:LND ?= {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
				}
				save_scope_as = the_louis_scope
				set_variable = {
					name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
					months = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.205.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:the_louis_scope = {
			remove_trait = sickly
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.205.b
		add_treasury = -1000
		scope:the_louis_scope = {
			remove_trait = sickly
		}
		c:LND ?= {
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
				exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
			}
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
				}
				exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					add_character_role = general
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = ecchi_chi_dsk_scholar_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				add_character_role = general
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = dsk_gbr_miss_louis_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

clever_dsk.243 = {
	#婚礼
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.243.t
	desc = clever_dsk.243.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.243.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = ROOT
	gui_window = event_window_2char_wedding_adventure
	left_icon = scope:aus_king_franz_scope
	right_icon = scope:aus_queen_xixi_scope
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/dual_monachy1.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/habsburg.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:AUS ?= this
			was_formed_from = AUS
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
			is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
		}
		game_date > 1855.12.24
		game_date < 1880.2.24
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = franz_and_xixi
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = com_internal_prestige_leader_old
		}		#社区
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = franz_and_xixi
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
			}
			save_scope_as = aus_king_franz_scope
		        v3sp_attach_historical_spouse_to_character_effect = yes
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
				months = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = aus_queen_elisabeth_von_wittelsbach
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = aus_queen_elisabeth_von_wittelsbach
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = aus_queen_elisabeth_von_wittelsbach
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = aus_queen_elisabeth_von_wittelsbach
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = aus_queen_elisabeth_von_wittelsbach
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = aus_queen_elisabeth_von_wittelsbach
			}
			save_scope_as = aus_queen_xixi_scope
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
				months = 1
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.243.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:aus_queen_xixi_scope = {
			add_trait = love_franz_and_xixi
		}
		scope:aus_king_franz_scope = {
			add_trait = love_franz_and_xixi
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.245 = {
	#隐藏事件，给历史角色增长退休日期，别睡
	type = country_event
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
			is_historical = yes
			is_immortal = no
		}
		game_date > 1836.1.2
		game_date < 1936.1.1
	}
	immediate = {
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				is_historical = yes
				is_immortal = no
				NOT = {
					has_role = character_role_historical_character
				}
			}
			add_character_role = character_role_historical_character
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
                                OR = {
                                AND = {
				is_historical = yes
				has_role = character_role_historical_character
                                }
				has_role = character_role_royal_queen
                                }
				is_immortal = no
			}
			add_career_length = {
				months = 24
				random_range = {
					1
					1.5
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.246 = {
	#婚礼
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.246.t
	desc = clever_dsk.246.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.246.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = ROOT
	gui_window = event_window_2char_wedding_adventure
	left_icon = root.ruler
	right_icon = scope:married_young_girl_scope
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_automobile"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/diet_of_hungary2.dds"
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = com_internal_prestige_leader_old
		}		#社区
	}
	immediate = {
		ruler ?= {
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
				months = 1
			}
		}
		ruler.interest_group ?= {
			save_scope_as = ruler_ig_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ruler ?= {
					is_female = no
				}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = character_role_royal_queen
                                NOT = { has_variable = is_married_young_girl }
				is_female = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = married_young_girl_scope
			set_variable = is_married_young_girl
		}
                }
		if = {
			limit = {
				ruler ?= {
					is_female = yes
				}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = character_role_royal_queen
                                NOT = { has_variable = is_married_young_boy }
				is_female = no
			}
			save_scope_as = married_young_girl_scope
			set_variable = is_married_young_boy
		}
                }
		scope:married_young_girl_scope = {
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
				months = 1
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.246.a
		default_option = yes
		ruler ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = love_another
			}
		}
		scope:married_young_girl_scope = {
			add_trait = love_another
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					add_trait = wrathful
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = ambitious
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = persistent
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = compliant
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = romantic
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = innovative
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = kind
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = wasteful
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = pious
				}
				10 = {
					add_trait = aesthete
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:FRA ?= this
			was_formed_from = FRA
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = fra_napoleon_iii_character_template
			is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
		}
		game_date > 1853.1.1
		game_date < 1870.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = nap_iii_and_eugenie
		}
		has_global_variable = com_internal_prestige_leader_old		#社区
	}

	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = nap_iii_and_eugenie
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = fra_napoleon_iii_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = fra_king_nap_iii_scope
		        v3sp_attach_historical_spouse_to_character_effect = yes
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
				months = 1
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_fra_eugenie_montijo_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = fra_eugenie_montijo_scope
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_wedding_hand
				months = 1
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.248.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:fra_king_nap_iii_scope = {
			add_trait = love_nap_and_eugenie
		}
		scope:fra_eugenie_montijo_scope = {
			add_trait = love_nap_and_eugenie
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

			}

clever_dsk.259 = {

	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	desc = clever_dsk.259.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.259.f
	duration = 1
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus

	left_icon = scope:jap_okubo_toshimichi_scope
	right_icon = scope:jap_kido_takayoshi_scope
        event_image = {
		video = "ep2_intellectual_writer"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/jap.dds"
	trigger = {
		c:JAP ?= this
		game_date > 1877.4.28
		game_date < 1877.7.28
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = kido_takayoshi_dead_now
		}

		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = ecchi_jap_kido_takayoshi_character_template
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = kido_takayoshi_dead_now
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
                        }
      			save_scope_as = kido_takayoshi_from_ig          
                }
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_jap_okubo_toshimichi_template
			}
			save_scope_as = jap_okubo_toshimichi_scope
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_smile
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_shengbi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_proud
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_chensi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_judao
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_angry
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_gongren
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_zhiyuan
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_jianyi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_nongmin
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_yinchen
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kongbu
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_pibei
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kelian
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_sad
                                days = 31
			}
                        }
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_jap_kido_takayoshi_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = jap_kido_takayoshi_scope
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_smile
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_sad
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_proud
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_chensi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_judao
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_angry
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_gongren
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_zhiyuan
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_jianyi
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_nongmin
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_yinchen
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kongbu
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_pibei
			remove_variable = ecchi_27_kelian
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_shengbi
                                days = 31
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = deathly_ill
					}
				}
				add_trait = deathly_ill
			}
                        }
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.259.a
		trigger = {
		}
		scope:jap_kido_takayoshi_scope = {
			kill_character = yes
		}

			value = 0.05

		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.259.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		trigger = {
		any_scope_character = {
				has_trait = doctor
			}
		}
		add_treasury = -20000
		scope:jap_kido_takayoshi_scope = {
			remove_trait = deathly_ill
                        remove_variable = ecchi_27_shengbi
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

	#第一次伊普尔战役
	type = country_event
	placement = s:STATE_FLANDERS.region_state:BEL
	title = clever_dsk.262.t
	desc = clever_dsk.262.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.262.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_highlander
	minor_left_icon = c:BEL
	minor_right_icon = c:GER
	left_icon = scope:bel_battle_of_ypres_benefactor
	right_icon = scope:ger_battle_of_ypres_benefactor
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_devastation"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:FRA ?= this
			c:GER ?= this
			c:GBR ?= this
			c:BEL ?= this
			is_player = yes
		}
		exists = c:FRA
		exists = c:BEL
		exists = c:GER
		game_date > 1914.7.1
		game_date < 1916.11.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = battle_of_ypres
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			has_war_with = c:FRA
			has_war_with = c:BEL
			any_scope_character = {
				OR = {
					has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
					has_template = pru_general_von_moltke
					has_template = wur_albert_wurttemberg
				}
			}
		}
		c:BEL ?= {
			has_war_with = c:GER
			any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
				        devastation >= 0.001
                        }
			any_scope_war = {
				any_scope_front = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_FLANDERS
					}
				}
			}
              }
		c:FRA ?= {
			any_scope_character = {
				OR = {
					has_template = fra_general_joffre
					has_template = fra_general_foch
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = battle_of_ypres
		if = {
			limit = {
		                     exists = c:BEL
			             c:BEL ?= this
		}
		s:STATE_FLANDERS = {
			start_harvest_condition = flood
		}
                }
		c:GER ?= {
			save_scope_as = ger_Battle_of_Ypres
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
					         has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
					         has_template = pru_general_von_moltke
					         has_template = wur_albert_wurttemberg
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = ger_battle_of_ypres_benefactor
			}
		}
		c:BEL ?= {
			save_scope_as = bel_Battle_of_Ypres
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
                                               is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = bel_battle_of_ypres_benefactor
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = fra_Battle_of_Ypres
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
					                 has_template = fra_general_joffre
					                 has_template = fra_general_foch
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = fra_battle_of_ypres_benefactor
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.262.a
		show_as_tooltip = {
		s:STATE_FLANDERS = {
			start_harvest_condition = flood
		}
		}
		c:GER ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = Artillery_Battle_of_Ypres
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = Artillery_Battle_of_Ypres
					months = 2
				}
			}
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {

					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = Artillery_Battle_of_Ypres
					months = 2
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

	placement = ROOT
