	desc = clever_dsk.264.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.264.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_highlander
	minor_left_icon = ROOT
	minor_right_icon = c:CSA
	left_icon = scope:usa_general_grant_benefactor
	right_icon = scope:csa_robbot_lee_benefactor
	event_image = {
		video = gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_american_civil_war.bk2
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag_usa_distress.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:USA
		c:USA = ROOT
		game_date > 1856.1.1
		game_date < 1863.1.1
		exists = c:CSA
		has_war_with = c:CSA
		NOT = {
			has_variable = usa_general_grant_inspiring
		}
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = usa_general_grant
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = usa_general_grant_inspiring
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = usa_general_grant
			}
			save_scope_as = usa_general_grant_benefactor
		add_modifier = {
				name = usfp_civil_war_hero
		}	
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = usa_general_lee
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = usa_general_lee
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = usa_general_lee
					}
			                exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
					transfer_character = c:CSA
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
		                        add_character_role = general
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = usa_general_lee
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = usa_general_lee
				}
				transfer_character = c:CSA
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
		                add_character_role = general
			}
		}
		else = {
                c:CSA = {
			create_character = {
				template = usa_general_lee
				ig_leader = yes
                                is_general = yes
			}
		}
		}
                c:CSA = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = usa_general_lee
			}
			save_scope_as = csa_robbot_lee_benefactor
		}
                }
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.264.a
		trigger = {
		}
		add_modifier = {
					name = battle_of_america_grant
					months = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.265 = {
	#呼叫加里波第
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.265.t
	desc = clever_dsk.265.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.265.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = ROOT
	gui_window = event_window_2char_adventure
	left_icon = scope:ita_camillo_gafur_benefactor
	right_icon = scope:garibaldi_scope
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/1848_Garibaldi.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:SAR ?= this
			was_formed_from = SAR
		}
		exists = c:SIC
		game_date > 1846.1.1
		game_date < 1866.1.1
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = ecchi_ita_camillo_gafur_template
		}
		OR = {
		any_country = {
		any_scope_character = {
					has_template = garibaldi_character_template
		}
		}
		any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = garibaldi_character_template
		}
                }
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = garibaldi_come_beacuse_gafur
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = garibaldi_come_beacuse_gafur
                ROOT = {
                save_scope_as = garibaldi_new_home
                }
		c:SIC ?= {
                save_scope_as = garibaldi_spawn_foe_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_ita_camillo_gafur_template
			}
			save_scope_as = ita_camillo_gafur_benefactor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = garibaldi_character_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = garibaldi_character_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = garibaldi_character_template
					}
		                        exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
		                        add_character_role = general
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = garibaldi_character_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = garibaldi_character_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
		                add_character_role = general
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = garibaldi_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.265.a
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					ROOT = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
					has_variable = garibaldi_has_been_here
				}
			}
			post_notification = garibaldi_is_here
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			culture = cu:north_italian
			pop_type = soldiers
			value = 0.35
		}
		scope:garibaldi_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = imperious_leader_good_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:ita_camillo_gafur_benefactor = {
			add_modifier = { 
				name = imperious_leader_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.265.b
		default_option = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					ROOT = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
					has_variable = garibaldi_has_been_here
				}
			}
			post_notification = garibaldi_is_here
		}

			pop_type = soldiers
			value = 0.15
		}

clever_dsk.266 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.266.t
	desc = clever_dsk.266.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.266.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_3char_selection
	left_icon = scope:giuseppe_mazzini_scope
	center_icon = scope:garibaldi_scope
	right_icon = scope:ita_camillo_gafur_benefactor
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_military_parade"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/ita2.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:ITA
		c:ITA = ROOT
		game_date > 1846.1.1
		game_date < 1870.1.1
		any_scope_character = { #加富尔
			has_template = ecchi_ita_camillo_gafur_template
		}
		any_scope_character = { #加里波第
			has_template = garibaldi_character_template
		}
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = italian_three_handsome_man
		}
	}
	immediate = {
                set_global_variable = italian_three_handsome_man
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
					}
		                        exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
				on_created = {
				set_career_length = {
						years = 10
						random_range = { 1.0 1.5 }
				}
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = agitator_giuseppe_mazzini
			}
			save_scope_as = giuseppe_mazzini_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = garibaldi_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = garibaldi_scope
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_ita_camillo_gafur_template
			}
			save_scope_as = ita_camillo_gafur_benefactor
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.266.a
		trigger = {
		}
		scope:giuseppe_mazzini_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
			}
		}
		scope:garibaldi_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
			}
		}
		scope:ita_camillo_gafur_benefactor = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popularity_person
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

clever_dsk.268 = {
	#大清不想打了
	type = country_event
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = {
                AND = {
                         exists = c:AUS
			 has_war_with = c:AUS
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:RUS
			 has_war_with = c:RUS
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:GER
			 has_war_with = c:GER
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:GBR
			 has_war_with = c:GBR
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:FRA
			 has_war_with = c:FRA
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:ITA
			 has_war_with = c:ITA
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:USA
			 has_war_with = c:USA
                }
                AND = {
                         exists = c:JAP
			 has_war_with = c:JAP
                }
                }
		is_at_war = yes
                is_player = no
	}
	immediate = {
			every_scope_war = {
                        limit = {
                                      is_warleader = ROOT
                                      OR = {
                                      is_warleader = c:AUS
                                      is_warleader = c:GER
                                      is_warleader = c:ITA
                                      is_warleader = c:JAP
                                      is_warleader = c:GBR
                                      is_warleader = c:FRA
                                      is_warleader = c:USA
                                      is_warleader = c:RUS
                        }
                        }
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = ROOT
					value = -190
				}
			}
	}
}

clever_dsk.269 = {
	#剑术大会
	type = country_event

        event_image = {
		video = "ep2_japanese_cityscape"
	}

	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/jap.dds"
	trigger = {
		exists = c:JAP
		c:JAP = ROOT

		game_date < 1936.1.1

			has_trait = fencer
                        commander_rank >= 3

		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = com_internal_prestige_leader_old
		}		#社区
	}

                        commander_rank >= 3
                        }

			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_kongbu
				months = 1
			}
		}
	       random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
                        age >= 18
			NOT = { this = scope:sword_i_japanese_scope }
                        OR = {
			has_trait = fencer
                        commander_rank >= 3
                        }
			}
			save_scope_as = sword_ii_japanese_scope

				name = ecchi_27_kongbu
				months = 1
			}
		}

	option = {

			if = {

					}

			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = i_return_triumphant
				days = 180
			}
                        }
		        scope:sword_ii_japanese_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = tarnished_honor_modifier
				days = 180
			}
                        }
                        }
			50 = {
		        scope:sword_ii_japanese_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = fencer
					}
				}
				add_trait = fencer
			}
			add_modifier = {

				days = 180
			}
                        }
                        }
